Independent feature branches (features/<name>), each based on illumos-gate
479a4a914dc and applicable on its own, then the octopus merge of all of
them (features-merged), whose tree is identical to illumos-joyent da512a7e2e6.
Regenerated by features.py.

[20/80] features/vnd

SmartOS: vnd: Project Bardiche (vnd, frameio, gsqueue, sdev plugins)

The vnd(4D) virtual network device, libvnd, vndadm/vndstat, generalized
squeues, and the libdlpi/snoop support for them.

Features:
  OS-610 snoop and libdlpi should be able to consume /dev/net/zone/%zonename
  OS-2544 ipf rules from the GZ should be add to in-zone rules, not replace them (man page and style fixes)
  OS-2780 Want dlpi primitive DL_EXCLUSIVE_REQ
  OS-2781 dlpi promisc modes should be able to be RX-only
  OS-2782 want /dev/net/zone/%zonename/%datalink
  OS-2783 want generalized squeues
  OS-2784 want sdev plugin interface
  OS-2785 ipf should be able to hook kvm nics
  OS-3203 want check target for verifying structures are the same in ILP32 and LP64
  OS-4839 Want ability to toggle etherstub link state
  OS-4840 Want system event for datalink state changes
  OS-6558 sdev_create_minor_node should allow non-666
  OS-7662 need a way to disable SMT
  OS-7943 Want platform build test artifact
  OS-8064 test-fw "enable / disable - enable zone fw" has "ipf -E" failing with SIOCFRENB error (#242)

Also includes the final state of fixes from 40 tickets: OS-830, OS-2778,
OS-2779, OS-2786, OS-2800, OS-2841, OS-2920, OS-3094, OS-3119, OS-3142,

[67/80] features/lx

SmartOS: lx brand: illumos-joyent changes on top of OmniOS

Brings the lx files from OmniOS's versions to illumos-joyent's, and adds
what OmniOS does not carry: the lx zone brand scripts and configuration,
the legacy lxproc file system, and the lx(7) and lxproc(4FS) manual
pages.

Features:
  OS-1119 add lxproc, a coarsely Linux-compatible /proc

[68/80] features/sed

SmartOS: sed: keep -i/-I with a required argument

Joyent reverted illumos 586 (sed -i optional argument, --in-place) in
2011 and has kept the older behavior.

Plus 1 commits without a ticket ID.

Notes:
  cmd/sed and sed(1) deliberately lack illumos 586 ("sed -i should take
  an optional argument"): joyent reverted it in 58474c20a52 (2011) and
  has kept the older behavior, where -i and -I require an argument and
  there is no --in-place.

2 files (0 removed). This commit applies to illumos-gate on its own;
files shared with other features carry only this feature's hunks.

Co-authored-by: John Sonnenschein <johns@joyent.com>
